Actually, the problem is the log format. The progress bar lines and the loss lines are on the same line sometimes, with the loss entry appearing after a bunch of `\r` characters and progress bars. Let me try a different approach - reading specific parts of the log where the loss entries should be.

Given that each epoch has ~478 steps and loss is logged every ~50 steps (28 entries / 3 epochs ≈ 9.3 per epoch), the loss entries are at roughly step 50 intervals.


I'm mapping out where the loss entries appear across the files - CWM and CLS both have their first entries mixed with progress bars around line 30, with epoch 0.1 losses visible around line 478, and later epoch losses clustered in the 885-926 line range. The core issue is that `\r` characters are embedding the loss data inline with the progress output, so I need to target specific line ranges to extract the actual values. Looking at the head output more carefully, I can see the first training loss entries are both at line 478 with epoch 0.1 — CWM shows a loss of 1.8751 and CLS shows 1.421, though the line appears truncated in the output.

The grep command I ran picked up learning_rate entries along with what I was looking for, so I'm only seeing fragments of the full data. I think I need a more targeted approach to extract just the loss values — maybe using grep to pull only the lines containing "loss" entries so I can get all 28 values for each experiment cleanly.
